# Continuation after pull request 201

This checkpoint carries unfinished work from [PR 201](https://github.com/link-foundation/meta-language/pull/201)
and [issue 199](https://github.com/link-foundation/meta-language/issues/199).
[The vision](../vision.md) remains the sole architecture contract. The complete
[atomic ledger](../../parity/issue-195-requirements.lino), its source register
and [the preceding handoff](issue-199-pr-201-2026-10-18.md) remain binding.
This continuation does not close either issue or claim full implementation.
The checkpoint accounts below are historical. The latest merge and retained
draft are described in [the issue 227 handoff](#issue-227-checkpoint-and-retained-draft-october-22);
the vision remains authoritative.

sole pusher. Use the shared checkout and bounded targeted JavaScript checks.

authenticated source receipts. Correct wrong assertions with documented
evidence and version bounds while retaining inputs and coverage.
